# PLAN-Rows — Alternative Set (Families 9–14, MIT/Apache 2.0)

Planning items for config-driven multi-vertical platforms (Genie DNA pattern).
Format mirrors `new sets/PLAN-Rows.md`.

```
Common coverage labels (file-constant):
  source_license_state     : external_license_checked
  contract_signature       : SchemePlannerUnit
  node_role                : layer_2 (PLAN)
  pair_kind                : flow_node
```
